export SPARK_LOCAL_IP=127.0.0.1
export SPARK_LOCAL_HOSTNAME=localhost
export PYSPARK_SUBMIT_ARGS="--conf spark.driver.bindAddress=127.0.0.1 --conf spark.driver.host=127.0.0.1 pyspark-shell"

df = spark.read.csv("hdfs:///user/vm_admin/data/ratings.csv", header=True, inferSchema=True)

df.groupBy("rating").count().show()


echo 'export SPARK_LOCAL_IP=127.0.0.1' >> ~/.bashrc
echo 'export SPARK_LOCAL_HOSTNAME=localhost' >> ~/.bashrc
echo 'export PYSPARK_SUBMIT_ARGS="--conf spark.driver.bindAddress=127.0.0.1 --conf spark.driver.host=127.0.0.1 pyspark-shell"' >> ~/.bashrc
source ~/.bashrc


from pyspark.sql.functions import from_unixtime


from pyspark.sql.functions import from_unixtime, to_timestamp, year, quarter, month, col

df2 = df.withColumn("review_time", to_timestamp(from_unixtime(col("timestamp")))) \
        .withColumn("review_year", year(col("review_time"))) \
        .withColumn("review_quarter", quarter(col("review_time"))) \
        .withColumn("review_month", month(col("review_time")))
		
df2.select("timestamp", "review_time", "review_year", "review_quarter", "review_month").show(5, False)

df = df.withColumn("date", from_unixtime(df.timestamp))
df.select("timestamp", "date").show(5)

most active users
df.groupBy("user_id").count().orderBy("count", ascending=False).show(10)

most rated products
df.groupBy("item_id").count().orderBy("count", ascending=False).show(10)


average rating per product
from pyspark.sql.functions import avg

df.groupBy("item_id").agg(avg("rating")).show(10)


rating trend over time
df.groupBy("date").count().orderBy("date").show()


filter high ratings
df.filter(df.rating >= 4).show(5)




q2_2014 = df2.filter((col("review_year") == 2014) & (col("review_quarter") == 2))
q2_2014.count()


high_group = q2_2014.filter(col("rating") >= 3)
low_group = q2_2014.filter(col("rating") < 3)

high_group.count()
low_group.count()


q2_2014.groupBy("rating").count().orderBy("rating").show()
high_group.select("item_id").distinct().count()
low_group.select("item_id").distinct().count()


high_product = high_group.groupBy("item_id").count().withColumnRenamed("count", "high_reviews")
low_product = low_group.groupBy("item_id").count().withColumnRenamed("count", "low_reviews")

high_product.describe().show()
low_product.describe().show()


high_product.orderBy("high_reviews", ascending=False).show(10)
low_product.orderBy("low_reviews", ascending=False).show(10)

q2_2014.groupBy("item_id").avg("rating").orderBy("avg(rating)", ascending=False).show(10)


high_vals = high_product.select("high_reviews").rdd.flatMap(lambda x: x).collect()
low_vals = low_product.select("low_reviews").rdd.flatMap(lambda x: x).collect()


from scipy import stats

t_stat, p_value = stats.ttest_ind(high_vals, low_vals, equal_var=False)

print("t-statistic:", t_stat)
print("p-value:", p_value)

A Welch’s t-test was conducted to compare the mean number of reviews between high-rated and low-rated products. The results showed a statistically significant difference (p < 0.05), indicating that high-rated products receive significantly more reviews on average.

cd ~
wget https://archive.apache.org/dist/kafka/3.7.0/kafka_2.13-3.7.0.tgz
tar -xzf kafka_2.13-3.7.0.tgz


cd ~/kafka_2.13-3.7.0
KAFKA_CLUSTER_ID="$(bin/kafka-storage.sh random-uuid)"
echo $KAFKA_CLUSTER_ID


bin/kafka-storage.sh format -t $KAFKA_CLUSTER_ID -c config/kraft/server.properties

bin/kafka-server-start.sh -daemon config/kraft/server.properties


bin/kafka-topics.sh --create \
  --topic reviews \
  --bootstrap-server localhost:9092 \
  --partitions 1 \
  --replication-factor 1
  
  

cd ~/bigdata_coursework

python3 - <<'PY'
import csv, json, itertools
with open('ratings.csv', newline='') as f, open('reviews_stream.json', 'w') as out:
    reader = csv.DictReader(f)
    for row in itertools.islice(reader, 1000):
        out.write(json.dumps(row) + '\n')
PY

head -3 ~/bigdata_coursework/reviews_stream.json
wc -l ~/bigdata_coursework/reviews_stream.json


cd ~
spark-shell \
  --packages org.apache.spark:spark-sql-kafka-0-10_2.12:3.5.0 \
  --conf spark.driver.bindAddress=127.0.0.1 \
  --conf spark.driver.host=127.0.0.1
  
val reviewSchema = new StructType().add("user_id", StringType).add("item_id", StringType).add("rating", StringType).add("timestamp", StringType)  

val rawKafka = spark.readStream.format("kafka").option("kafka.bootstrap.servers", "localhost:9092").option("subscribe", "reviews").option("startingOffsets", "earliest").load()  

val parsed = rawKafka.selectExpr("CAST(value AS STRING) as json_str").select(from_json(col("json_str"), reviewSchema).as("data")).select("data.*").withColumn("rating", col("rating").cast("double")).withColumn("event_time", to_timestamp(from_unixtime(col("timestamp").cast("long"))))

val agg = parsed.groupBy("item_id").agg(count("*").as("review_count"),round(avg("rating"), 2).as("avg_rating"))

val consoleQuery = agg.writeStream.outputMode("complete").format("console").option("truncate", "false").start()
  
val rawToHdfs = parsed.writeStream.format("parquet").option("path", "hdfs:///user/vm_admin/output/reviews_raw").option("checkpointLocation", "hdfs:///user/vm_admin/checkpoints/reviews_raw").outputMode("append").start()



hdfs dfs -ls /user/vm_admin/output/reviews_raw

val rawToHdfs = parsed.writeStream.format("parquet").option("path", "hdfs://localhost:9000/user/vm_admin/output/reviews_raw").option("checkpointLocation", "hdfs://localhost:9000/user/vm_admin/checkpoints/reviews_raw").outputMode("append").start()



  
  
  cd ~/kafka_2.13-3.7.0
bin/kafka-console-producer.sh --bootstrap-server localhost:9092 --topic reviews < ~/bigdata_coursework/reviews_stream.json


spark.streams.active.foreach(_.processAllAvailable())


consoleQuery.stop()
rawToHdfs.stop()
